||\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}m_{3,t}^{\bm{\theta}}(\bar{\mathbf{X}}_{t}(\bm{\upsilon}))||_{2}\leq C\sum_{m=1}^{J}|\nabla_{x_{m}}\log f_{m}(Y_{t};\bar{X}_{m,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{m}),\bm{\upsilon}_{m})|.

Hence, we have that

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}}\sup_{\mathbf{s}\in\mathcal{S}}|[\nabla_{\mathbf{s}}[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\mathbf{s};\bm{\theta})]_{j}-\nabla_{\mathbf{s}}[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\mathbf{s};\bm{\theta})]_{j}]_{r}|

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{m,n=1}^{J}\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{m}\in\bm{\Upsilon}_{m}}\sup_{x_{m}\in\mathcal{X}_{m}}|\nabla_{x_{m}}\log f_{m}(Y_{t};x_{m},\bm{\upsilon}_{m})|\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{n}\in\bm{\Upsilon}_{n}}|\hat{X}_{n,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{n})-X_{n,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{n})|

for all
j\in\{1,...,J\}
and
r\in\{1,...,J\}
. Condition (iii) thus follows by using the same arguments as above.

A.3
Proof of Theorem
2

The conclusion follows from Lemmas 3.1 and 4.1 in
Pötscher and Prucha (1997)
if

(i)

\bm{\Theta}
is compact,

(ii)

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}}|\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})-L(\bm{\theta})|\overset{a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
T\rightarrow\infty
,

(iii)

\bm{\theta}\mapsto L(\bm{\theta})
is continuous, and

(iv)

L(\bm{\theta})\leq L(\bm{\theta}_{0})
for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}
with equality if and only if
\bm{\theta}=\bm{\theta}_{0}
.

Condition (i) follows from Assumption
2
. Condition (ii) follows from Lemmas
A.4
and
A.5
since

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}}|\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})-L(\bm{\theta})|\leq\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}}|\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})-L_{T}(\bm{\theta})|+\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}}|L_{T}(\bm{\theta})-L(\bm{\theta})|

and Condition (iii) is a by-product of the uniform law of large numbers used in the proof of Lemma
A.5
.

Lemma A.4
.

Under the assumptions in Theorem
2
,

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}}|\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})-L_{T}(\bm{\theta})|\overset{a.s.}{\rightarrow}0\quad\text{as}\quad T\rightarrow\infty.

Proof.

We have that

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}}|\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})-L_{T}(\bm{\theta})|\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}}|\log\hat{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})-\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})|.

The conclusion thus follows from Lemma 2.1 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
if
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}}|\log\hat{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})-\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})|\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
.

We have that

|\log\hat{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})-\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})|\leq|\log\hat{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})-\log\tilde{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})|+|\log\tilde{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})-\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})|,

(A.8)

where
\tilde{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})=\sum_{j=1}^{J}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{j}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})
. For the first term on the right-hand side of Equation (
A.8
), let
m_{1,t}^{\bm{\theta}}:\mathcal{S}_{\bm{\theta}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
be given by

m_{1,t}^{\bm{\theta}}(\mathbf{s})=\log\sum_{j=1}^{J}s_{j}f_{j}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j}).

Then, by the mean value theorem and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, there exists a
\bar{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\in\mathcal{S}_{\bm{\theta}}
such that

|m_{1,t}^{\bm{\theta}}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}))-m_{1,t}^{\bm{\theta}}(\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}))|\leq C||\nabla_{\mathbf{s}}m_{1,t}^{\bm{\theta}}(\bar{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}))||_{2}||\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})-\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})||_{2},

where

\displaystyle|[\nabla_{\mathbf{s}}m_{1,t}^{\bm{\theta}}(\bar{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}))]_{k}|

\displaystyle=\frac{f_{k}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})}{\sum_{j=1}^{J}\bar{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{j}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\bar{\pi}_{k,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})}\frac{\bar{\pi}_{k,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{k}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})}{\sum_{j=1}^{J}\bar{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{j}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})}

\displaystyle\leq C

by Remark
1
so

||\nabla_{\mathbf{s}}m_{1,t}^{\bm{\theta}}(\bar{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}))||_{2}\leq C.

For the second term on the right-hand side of Equation (
A.8
), let
m_{2,t}^{\bm{\theta}}:\mathcal{X}_{1}\times\cdots\times\mathcal{X}_{J}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
be given by

m_{2,t}^{\bm{\theta}}(\mathbf{x})=\log\sum_{j=1}^{J}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j}).

Again, by the mean value theorem and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, there exists an
\bar{\mathbf{X}}_{t}(\mathbf{\bm{\upsilon}})\in\mathcal{X}_{1}\times\cdots\times\mathcal{X}_{J}
such that

|m_{2,t}^{\bm{\theta}}(\hat{\mathbf{X}}_{t}(\mathbf{\bm{\upsilon}}))-m_{2,t}^{\bm{\theta}}(\mathbf{X}_{t}(\mathbf{\bm{\upsilon}}))|\leq||\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}m_{2,t}^{\bm{\theta}}(\bar{\mathbf{X}}_{t}(\mathbf{\bm{\upsilon}}))||_{2}||\hat{\mathbf{X}}_{t}(\mathbf{\bm{\upsilon}})-\mathbf{X}_{t}(\mathbf{\bm{\upsilon}})||_{2},

where

\displaystyle|[\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}m_{2,t}^{\bm{\theta}}(\bar{\mathbf{X}}_{t}(\mathbf{\bm{\upsilon}}))]_{k}|

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi_{k,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})|\nabla_{x_{k}}f_{k}(Y_{t};\bar{X}_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})|}{\sum_{j=1}^{J}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{j}(Y_{t};\bar{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})}

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi_{k,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{k}(Y_{t};\bar{X}_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})}{\sum_{j=1}^{J}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{j}(Y_{t};\bar{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})}|\nabla_{x_{k}}\log f_{k}(Y_{t};\bar{X}_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})|

\displaystyle\leq|\nabla_{x_{k}}\log f_{k}(Y_{t};\bar{X}_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})|

so

||\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}m_{2,t}^{\bm{\theta}}(\bar{\mathbf{X}}_{t}(\mathbf{\bm{\upsilon}}))||_{2}\leq\sum_{k=1}^{J}\left|\nabla_{x_{k}}\log f_{k}(Y_{t};\bar{X}_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})\right|.

Hence, we have that

\displaystyle|\log\hat{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})-\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})|

\displaystyle\leq C||\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})-\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k,l=1}^{J}|\nabla_{x_{k}}\log f_{k}(Y_{t};\bar{X}_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})||\hat{X}_{l,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{l})-X_{l,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{l})|.

It thus follows from Lemmas 2.1 and 2.2 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
that
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}}|\log\hat{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})-\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})|\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
since
(Y_{t})_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary by Assumption
1
, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
, there exists an
m_{j}>0
such that
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bm{\Upsilon}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}\left|\nabla_{x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\right|^{m_{j}}\right]<\infty
by assumption, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bm{\Upsilon}_{j}}|\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})-X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
by Lemma
1
, and
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}}||\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})-\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
by Corollary
1
.
∎

Lemma A.5
.

Under the assumptions in Theorem
2
,

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}}|L_{T}(\bm{\theta})-L(\bm{\theta})|\overset{a.s.}{\rightarrow}0\quad\text{as}\quad T\rightarrow\infty.

Proof.

The conclusion follows from the uniform law of large numbers by
Rao (1962)
if
(\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}}|\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})|\right]<\infty
.

First,
(\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}
since
(Y_{t})_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic by Assumption
1
, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
(X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic for all
\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bm{\Upsilon}_{j}
by Lemma
1
, and
(\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}
by Corollary
1
.

We have that

|\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})|\leq\sum_{j=1}^{J}|\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|

since

\displaystyle\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})

\displaystyle\geq\log\min_{j\in\{1,...,J\}}f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

\displaystyle=\min_{j\in\{1,...,J\}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

\displaystyle\geq\min_{j\in\{1,...,J\}}-|\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|\geq-\sum_{j=1}^{J}|\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|

and

\displaystyle\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})

\displaystyle\leq\log\max_{j\in\{1,...,J\}}f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

\displaystyle=\max_{j\in\{1,...,J\}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j\in\{1,...,J\}}|\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|\leq\sum_{j=1}^{J}|\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|.

Hence,
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}}|\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})|\right]<\infty
since, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bm{\Upsilon}_{j}}|\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|\right]<\infty
by Assumption
5
.
∎

Finally, Condition (iv) follows from Lemma
A.6
.

Lemma A.6
.

Under the assumptions in Theorem
2
,

L(\bm{\theta})\leq L(\bm{\theta}_{0})

for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}
with equality if and only if
\bm{\theta}=\bm{\theta}_{0}
.

Proof.

We have that

L(\bm{\theta})-L(\bm{\theta}_{0})=\mathbb{E}\left[\log\frac{f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})}{f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right].

Observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\log\frac{f^{(m)}(\mathbf{Y}_{t-m+1}^{t};\bm{\theta})}{f^{(m)}(\mathbf{Y}_{t-m+1}^{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\log\prod_{i=1}^{m}\frac{f(Y_{t-i+1};\bm{\theta})}{f(Y_{t-i+1};\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\log\frac{f(Y_{t-i+1};\bm{\theta})}{f(Y_{t-i+1};\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right]=m\mathbb{E}\left[\log\frac{f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})}{f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right],

so

\mathbb{E}\left[\log\frac{f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})}{f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right]=\frac{1}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\log\frac{f^{(m)}(\mathbf{Y}_{t-m+1}^{t};\bm{\theta})}{f^{(m)}(\mathbf{Y}_{t-m+1}^{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right].

Note that
\log x\leq x-1
for all
x>0
with equality if and only if
x=1
. Thus,

\mathbb{E}\left[\log\frac{f^{(m)}(\mathbf{Y}_{t-m+1}^{t};\bm{\theta})}{f^{(m)}(\mathbf{Y}_{t-m+1}^{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right]\leq 0

for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}
with equality if and only if
f^{(m)}(\mathbf{Y}_{t-m+1}^{t};\bm{\theta})=f^{(m)}(\mathbf{Y}_{t-m+1}^{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})
a.s. Hence, we have that

L(\bm{\theta})-L(\bm{\theta}_{0})\leq 0

for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bm{\Theta}
with equality if and only if
\bm{\theta}=\bm{\theta}_{0}
by Assumption
6
.
∎

A.4
Proof of Theorem
3

The conclusion follows from Lemma 8.1 in
Pötscher and Prucha (1997)
if

(i)

\bm{\theta}_{0}\in\textup{int}(\bm{\Theta})
,

(ii)

\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{T}\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\rightarrow}\bm{\theta}_{0}
as
T\rightarrow\infty
,

(iii)

\bm{\theta}\mapsto\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})
is twice continuously differentiable a.s.,

(iv)

\sqrt{T}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta}_{0})\overset{d}{\rightarrow}\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},\mathbf{I}(\bm{\theta}_{0}))
as
T\rightarrow\infty
,

(v)

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})-(-\mathbf{I}(\bm{\theta}))||_{2,2}\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\rightarrow}0
as
T\rightarrow\infty
, and

(vi)

\mathbf{I}(\bm{\theta}_{0})
is invertible.

First, Condition (i) follows from Assumption
7
, Condition (ii) from Theorem
2
, and Condition (iii) follows from Assumptions
8
and
9
. Moreover, Condition (iv) follows from Lemmas
A.7
,
A.8
, and
A.9
.

Lemma A.7
.

Under the assumptions in Theorem
3
,

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\sqrt{T}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})-\sqrt{T}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}L_{T}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}\overset{a.s.}{\rightarrow}0\quad\text{as}\quad T\rightarrow\infty.

Proof.

We have that

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\sqrt{T}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})-\sqrt{T}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}L_{T}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2},

where
\hat{f}_{t}
denotes
\hat{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})
and
f_{t}
denotes
f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})
and that

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{f}_{t}}{{f}_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{J}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{J}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2},

where
\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}
denotes
\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})
,
\hat{f}_{j,t}
denotes
f_{j}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{j,t},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})
,
\hat{X}_{j,t}
denotes
\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})
,
{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}
denotes
{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})
,
{f}_{j,t}
denotes
f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})
, and
{X}_{j,t}
denotes
{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})
.

As in the proof of Lemma
A.4
, the conclusion thus follows from Lemma 2.1 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
if
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
. This follows if, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
and
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
.

First,

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}\left|\frac{\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{{f}_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|

\displaystyle+C\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}\left|\frac{\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{{f}_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|.

We have that

\left|\frac{\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{{f}_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\leq\left|\frac{\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\tilde{f}_{t}}\right|+\left|\frac{\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\tilde{f}_{t}}-\frac{{f}_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|,

where
\tilde{f}_{t}
denotes
\tilde{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})=\sum_{j=1}^{J}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{j}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})
. By using the same arguments as in the proof of Lemma
A.4
, we have that

\left|\frac{\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{{f}_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\leq C\left|\left|\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}-\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}+C\sum_{k,l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{k}}\log\bar{f}_{k,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|,

where
\bar{f}_{j,t}
denotes
f_{j}(Y_{t};\bar{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})
. It thus follows from Lemmas 2.1 and 2.2 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
that, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
since, in addition to the arguments used in the proof of Lemma
A.4
, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}||_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
where
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1})_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}||_{2}^{2}\right]<\infty
by Lemma
5
.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}-{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\right|\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left|\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}-{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\right|\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2},

where

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}.

First, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2},

where

\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\leq\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\tilde{f}_{t}}\right|+\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\tilde{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|.

By using the same arguments as in the proof of Lemma
A.4
again, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log\hat{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\left|\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}-\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\sum_{l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|

\displaystyle+C\sum_{k,l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{k}}\log\bar{f}_{k,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|.

Second, we have that

\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}\leq\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\tilde{f}_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}+\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\tilde{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}.

By using the same arguments as in the proof of Lemma
A.4
once again, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{h=1}^{d_{j}}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}}\log\hat{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\left|\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}-\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\sum_{h=1}^{d_{j}}\sum_{l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}x_{j}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|

\displaystyle+C\sum_{h=1}^{d_{j}}\sum_{k,l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{k}}\log\bar{f}_{k,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|.

It thus follows from Lemmas 2.1 and 2.2 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
that, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
, also
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
since, in addition to the arguments used in the proof of Lemma
A.4
, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t}-\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}||_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
where
(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}{X}_{j,t})_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary for all
\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}{X}_{j,t}||_{2}\right]<\infty
by Lemma
3
. Moreover, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
, there exists an
m_{j}>0
such that
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}\left|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\log f_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2}^{m_{j}}\right]<\infty
,
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j,t}\right|^{m_{j}}\right]<\infty
, and
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}\left|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2}^{m_{j}}\right]<\infty
by assumption.
∎

Lemma A.8
.

Under the assumptions in Theorem
3
,

\sqrt{T}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}L_{T}(\bm{\theta}_{0})\overset{d}{\rightarrow}\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0},\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\right]\right)\quad\text{as}\quad T\rightarrow\infty.

Proof.

The conclusion follows from the central limit theorem by
Billingsley (1961)
together with the Cramér–Wold theorem if
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is a stationary and ergodic martingale difference sequence and
\mathbb{E}\left[||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})||_{2}^{2}\right]<\infty
.

First,
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is a stationary and ergodic martingale difference sequence since, in addition to the arguments used in the proof of Lemma
A.5
, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic for all
\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}
by Lemma
3
, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}
by Lemma
5
, and

\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\mid\mathbf{Y}_{-\infty}^{t-1}\right]=\int_{\mathcal{Y}}\left.\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f(y;\bm{\theta})\right|_{\bm{\theta}=\bm{\theta}_{0}}\textup{d}y=\left.\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}f(y;\bm{\theta})\textup{d}y\right|_{\bm{\theta}=\bm{\theta}_{0}}=\mathbf{0}.

We have that

||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})||_{2}^{2}=\frac{1}{f^{2}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})||_{2}^{2}

where

\displaystyle||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i,j=1}^{J}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{i,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{0})||_{2}f_{i}(Y_{t};X_{i,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{i,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{i,0})||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{0})||_{2}f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})

\displaystyle+2\sum_{i,j=1}^{J}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{i,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{0})||_{2}f_{i}(Y_{t};X_{i,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{i,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{i,0})\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{0})||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i,j=1}^{J}\pi_{i,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{0})||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{i}(Y_{t};X_{i,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{i,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{i,0})||_{2}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{0})||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2},

so

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})||_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{j=1}^{J}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{0})||_{2}^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+C\sum_{i,j=1}^{J}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{i,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{0})||_{2}^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+\left(\sum_{j=1}^{J}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\right)^{2}.

Moreover, we have that

||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}^{2}=\frac{1}{f_{j}^{2}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}^{2}

where

\displaystyle||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})\right|^{2}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})\right|||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}

\displaystyle+||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}^{2},

so

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})\right|^{2}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+2\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})\right|^{2}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\mathbb{E}\left[||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j,0})||_{2}^{2}\right].

Hence,
\mathbb{E}\left[||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})||_{2}^{2}\right]<\infty
since, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}^{2}\right]<\infty
by Lemma
5
and, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\right|^{2}||\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}\right]<\infty
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}\right]<\infty
by Assumption
10
.
∎

Lemma A.9
.

Under the assumptions in Theorem
3
,

\mathbf{I}(\bm{\theta}_{0})=\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\right].

Proof.

We have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\mid\mathbf{Y}_{-\infty}^{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\mid\mathbf{Y}_{-\infty}^{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathcal{Y}}\left.\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f(y;\bm{\theta})\right|_{\bm{\theta}=\bm{\theta}_{0}}\textup{d}y

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\mid\mathbf{Y}_{-\infty}^{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle+\left.\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}f(y;\bm{\theta})\textup{d}y\right|_{\bm{\theta}=\bm{\theta}_{0}}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\mid\mathbf{Y}_{-\infty}^{t-1}\right]

and thus, by the law of total expectation, that

\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\right]=-\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}_{0})\right].

∎

Condition (v) follows from Lemmas
A.10
and
A.11
since

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})-(-\mathbf{I}(\bm{\theta}))||_{2,2}\leq\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}L_{T}(\bm{\theta})||_{2,2}+\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}L_{T}(\bm{\theta})-(-\mathbf{I}(\bm{\theta}))||_{2,2}.

Lemma A.10
.

Under the assumptions in Theorem
3
,

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}L_{T}(\bm{\theta})||_{2,2}\overset{a.s.}{\rightarrow}0\quad\text{as}\quad T\rightarrow\infty.

Proof.

We have that

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}L_{T}(\bm{\theta})||_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\hat{f}_{t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{t}}{f_{t}}\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}f_{t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2},

where
\hat{f}_{t}
denotes
\hat{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})
and
f_{t}
denotes
f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})
, that

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\hat{f}_{t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{t}}{f_{t}}\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}f_{t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2},

and that

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{J}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{j=1}^{J}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{J}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2},

where
\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}
denotes
\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})
,
\hat{f}_{j,t}
denotes
f_{j}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{j,t},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})
,
\hat{X}_{j,t}
denotes
\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})
,
{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}
denotes
{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})
,
{f}_{j,t}
denotes
f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})
, and
{X}_{j,t}
denotes
{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})
.

The conclusion thus follows from Lemma 2.1 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
if
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\hat{f}_{t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{t}}{f_{t}}\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}f_{t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
and
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
. The former follows from the arguments used in the proof of Lemma
A.7
and the latter follows if, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
,
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
, and
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
.

First,

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2,2}\left|\frac{\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|

\displaystyle+C\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle+\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2,2}\left|\frac{\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|.

It thus follows from Lemmas 2.1 and 2.2 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
that, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
since, in addition to the arguments used in the proofs of Lemmas
A.4
and
A.7
, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}||_{2,2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
where
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1})_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}||_{2,2}\right]<\infty
by Lemma
6
.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}.

It thus follows from the arguments used in the proof of Lemma
A.7
that, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
, also
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
.

Finally,

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}-\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\right|\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle+\left|\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}-\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\right|\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle+\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2},

where

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\hat{X}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}f_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}{X}_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle+\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle+2\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\hat{X}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}f_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}X_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle+\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}.

First, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\hat{X}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}f_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}{X}_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\hat{X}_{j,t}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}{X}_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\hat{X}_{j,t}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}{X}_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2,2},

where

\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\leq\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\tilde{f}_{t}}\right|+\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\tilde{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|

where
\tilde{f}_{t}
denotes
\tilde{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})=\sum_{j=1}^{J}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{j}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})
and

\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\hat{X}_{j,t}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}{X}_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2,2}\leq\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}+2\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2}.

By using the same arguments as in the proof of Lemma
A.4
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\log\hat{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\left|\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}-\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log\hat{f}_{j,t}\right|^{2}\left|\left|\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}-\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\sum_{l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}x_{j}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|

\displaystyle+C\sum_{k,l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{k}}\log\bar{f}_{k,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|

\displaystyle+C\sum_{k,l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right|^{2}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{k}}\log\bar{f}_{k,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|,

where
\bar{f}_{j,t}
denotes
f_{j}(Y_{t};\bar{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})
. Second, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2,2},

where

\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\leq\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\tilde{f}_{t}}\right|+\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\tilde{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|

as in the proof of Lemma
A.7
. Third, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}\hat{X}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}f_{j,t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{\prime}}X_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}X_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}X_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{X}_{j,t}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}X_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2},

where

\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}\leq\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\tilde{f}_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}+\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\tilde{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}.

By using the same arguments as in the proof of Lemma
A.4
again, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{h=1}^{d_{j}}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}x_{j}}\log\hat{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\left|\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}-\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\sum_{h=1}^{d_{j}}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}}\log\hat{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log\hat{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\left|\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}-\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\sum_{h=1}^{d_{j}}\sum_{l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}x_{j}x_{j}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|

\displaystyle+C\sum_{h=1}^{d_{j}}\sum_{k,l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}x_{j}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{k}}\log\bar{f}_{k,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|

\displaystyle+C\sum_{h=1}^{d_{j}}\sum_{l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|

\displaystyle+C\sum_{h=1}^{d_{j}}\sum_{k,l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{k}}\log\bar{f}_{k,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|.

Finally, we have that

\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}\leq\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\tilde{f}_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}+\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\tilde{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}.

By using the same arguments as in the proof of Lemma
A.4
once again, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{h,i=1}^{d_{j}}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{i}}\log\hat{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\left|\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}-\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\sum_{h,i=1}^{d_{j}}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}}\log\hat{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{i}}\log\hat{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\left|\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t-1}-\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t-1}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\sum_{h,i=1}^{d_{j}}\sum_{l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{i}x_{j}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|

\displaystyle+C\sum_{h,i=1}^{d_{j}}\sum_{k,l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{i}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{k}}\log\bar{f}_{k,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|

\displaystyle+C\sum_{h,i=1}^{d_{j}}\sum_{l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}x_{j}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{i}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|

\displaystyle+C\sum_{h,i=1}^{d_{j}}\sum_{l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{i}x_{j}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|

\displaystyle+C\sum_{h,i=1}^{d_{j}}\sum_{k,l=1}^{J}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{h}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{j}]_{i}}\log\bar{f}_{j,t}\right|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{k}}\log\bar{f}_{k,t}\right||\hat{X}_{l,t}-X_{l,t}|.

It thus follows from Lemmas 2.1 and 2.2 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
that, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
, also
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\left|\frac{\hat{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{f}_{j,t}}{\hat{f}_{t}}-\frac{\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j,t}}{f_{t}}\right|\right|_{2,2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
since, in addition to the arguments used in the proofs of Lemmas
A.4
and
A.7
, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t}-\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}||_{2,2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
where
(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}{X}_{j,t})_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary for all
\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}{X}_{j,t}||_{2,2}\right]<\infty
by Lemma
4
. Moreover, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
, there exists an
m_{j}>0
such that
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}\left|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\log f_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2,2}^{m_{j}}\right]<\infty
,
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}\left|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j,t}\right|^{m_{j}}\right]<\infty
,
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}\left|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}x_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2}^{m_{j}}\right]<\infty
, and
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}\left|\left|\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j,t}\right|\right|_{2,2}^{m_{j}}\right]<\infty
by assumption.
∎

Lemma A.11
.

Under the assumptions in Theorem
3
,

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}L_{T}(\bm{\theta})-(-\mathbf{I}(\bm{\theta}))||_{2,2}\overset{a.s.}{\rightarrow}0\quad\text{as}\quad T\rightarrow\infty.

Proof.

As in the proof of Lemma
A.5
, the conclusion follows from the uniform law of large numbers by
Rao (1962)
if
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})||_{2,2}\right]<\infty
.

First,
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}
since, in addition to the arguments used in the proofs of Lemmas
A.5
and
A.8
, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic for all
\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}
by Lemma
4
and, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}
by Lemma
6
.

We have that

||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})||_{2,2}\leq\frac{1}{{f}^{2}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})||_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})||_{2,2}

where

\displaystyle||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})||_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{J}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})||_{2,2}f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

\displaystyle+2\sum_{j=1}^{J}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{J}{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2},

so

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})||_{2,2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log{f}(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})||_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+C\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})||_{2,2}\right]

\displaystyle+C\sum_{j=1}^{J}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}\right].

Moreover, we have that

\displaystyle||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{f_{j}^{2}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}

where

\displaystyle||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}

\displaystyle+2||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}

\displaystyle+||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2},

so

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|^{2}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}\right]

\displaystyle+2\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|^{2}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+2\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}\right].

Hence,
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\log f(Y_{t};\bm{\theta})||_{2,2}\right]<\infty
since, in addition to the arguments used in the proof of Lemma
A.8
, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}{\pi}_{j,t\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})||_{2,2}\right]<\infty
by Lemma
6
and, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|||\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}\right]<\infty
,
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|||\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}\right]<\infty
,
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}||\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}\right]<\infty
, and
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}\right]<\infty
by Assumption
10
.
∎

Finally, Condition (vi) is true by assumption.

A.5
Proof of Proposition
1

Note that

\displaystyle||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{L}_{T}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{T})-(-\mathbf{I}(\bm{\theta}_{0}))||_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}L_{T}(\bm{\theta})||_{2,2}

\displaystyle+\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}L_{T}(\bm{\theta})-(-\mathbf{I}(\bm{\theta}))||_{2,2}

\displaystyle+||(-\mathbf{I}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{T}))-(-\mathbf{I}(\bm{\theta}_{0}))||_{2,2}.

The conclusion thus follows since
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{L}_{T}(\bm{\theta})-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}L_{T}(\bm{\theta})||_{2,2}\overset{a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
T\rightarrow\infty
by Lemma
A.10
,
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}L_{T}(\bm{\theta})-(-\mathbf{I}(\bm{\theta}))||_{2,2}\overset{a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
T\rightarrow\infty
by Lemma
A.11
, and, by the continuous mapping theorem,
||(-\mathbf{I}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{T}))-(-\mathbf{I}(\bm{\theta}_{0}))||_{2,2}\overset{a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
T\rightarrow\infty
since
\bm{\theta}\mapsto(-\mathbf{I}(\bm{\theta}))
is continuous by the uniform law of large numbers used in the proof of Lemma
A.11
and
\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{T}\overset{a.s.}{\rightarrow}\bm{\theta}_{0}
as
T\rightarrow\infty
by Theorem
2
.

Other Proofs

B.1
Proof of Lemma
3

For each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is a stochastic process taking values in
\mathbb{R}^{d_{j}}
given by

\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t+1}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})=\mathbb{\phi}_{j,t}^{x}(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

with

\mathbb{\phi}_{j,t}^{x}(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})=\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})+\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j}).

The first conclusion thus follows from Theorem 2.10 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
since, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,

(i)

\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}]<\infty
,

(ii)

\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|]<\infty
, and

(iii)

-\infty\leq\mathbb{E}[\log\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|]<0

by assumption.
10
10

10

Conditions (ii) and (iii) follow from Conditions (ii) and (iii) in Lemma
1
.

Moreover, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}))_{t\in\mathbb{N}}
is a stochastic process taking values in
\mathbb{R}^{d_{j}}
given by

\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t+1}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})=\hat{\mathbb{\phi}}_{j,t}^{x}(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

with

\hat{\mathbb{\phi}}_{j,t}^{x}(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})=\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})+\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j}).

The second conclusion thus follows from Theorem 2.10 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
as well since, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,

(i)

\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}]<\infty
,

(ii)

\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})-\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
, and

(iii)

\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})-\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty

by assumption.

B.2
Proof of Lemma
4

For each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is a stochastic process taking values in
\mathbb{R}^{d_{j}\times d_{j}}
given by

\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t+1}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})=\mathbb{\phi}_{j,t}^{xx}(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

with

\displaystyle\mathbb{\phi}_{j,t}^{xx}(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

\displaystyle=\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})+\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}^{\prime}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

\displaystyle+\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})+\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}^{\prime}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

\displaystyle+\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j}).

The first conclusion thus follows from Theorem 2.10 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
since, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,

(i)

\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|]<\infty
,
\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}]<\infty
and
\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}]<\infty
,

(ii)

\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|]<\infty
, and

(iii)

-\infty\leq\mathbb{E}[\log\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|]<0

by assumption.

Moreover, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}))_{t\in\mathbb{N}}
is a stochastic process taking values in
\mathbb{R}^{d_{j}\times d_{j}}
given by

\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t+1}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})=\hat{\mathbb{\phi}}_{j,t}^{xx}(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

with

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{\phi}}_{j,t}^{xx}(\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

\displaystyle=\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})+\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}^{\prime}}\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

\displaystyle+\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})+\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}^{\prime}}\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})

\displaystyle+\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j}).

The second conclusion thus follows from Theorem 2.10 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
as well since, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,

(i)

\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}]<\infty
,

(ii)

\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})-\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
,
\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})-\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
and
\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})-\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
, and

(iii)

\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})-\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\phi_{j}(Y_{t},X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j});\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty

by assumption.
