11

Condition (iii) follows from Condition (iii) in Lemma
3
.

B.3
Proof of Lemma
5

The conclusion follows from Lemma
2
and the following lemma.

Lemma B.1
.

Assume that Assumptions
1
-
4
,
8
-
10
, and the conditions in Lemmas
1
-
3
hold. Moreover, assume that for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
, there exists an
m_{j}>0
such that

(i)

\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{m_{j}}]<\infty
,

(ii)

\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|^{m_{j}}]<\infty
, and

(iii)

\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{m_{j}}]<\infty
.

Then,
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}
with
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}^{2}]<\infty
and

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0\quad\text{as}\quad t\rightarrow\infty

for any initialisation
\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{0\mid 0}(\bm{\theta})\in\mathbb{R}^{(J-1)d}
.

Proof.

First,
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is a stochastic process taking values in
\mathbb{R}^{(J-1)d}
given by

\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})=\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})

with

\displaystyle\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{(n-1)d+m}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{J-1}\left(\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{j}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}\right)\left[\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\right]_{(j-1)d+m}

\displaystyle+\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n},\quad(n,m)\in\{1,...,J-1\}\times\{1,...,d\};

see Section
C
for details. Let

\Lambda(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}};\bm{\theta})=\sup_{\underset{\mathbf{x}\neq\mathbf{y}}{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{(J-1)d}}}\frac{||\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}}(\mathbf{x};\bm{\theta})-\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}}(\mathbf{y};\bm{\theta})||_{2}}{||\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{y}||_{2}}.

The fact that
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}
with
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}^{2}]<\infty
follows from Theorem 2.10 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
if

(i)

\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}}(\mathbf{0};\bm{\theta})||_{2}]<\infty
,

(ii)

\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\Lambda(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}};\bm{\theta})]<\infty
, and

(iii)

there exists an
r\in\mathbb{N}
such that
-\infty\leq\mathbb{E}[\log\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\Lambda((\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}})^{(r)};\bm{\theta})]<0
.

The former is a direct consequence of the theorem, and the latter is a bi-product of the theorem; see later. Condition (i) follows if, for each
(n,m)\in\{1,...,J-1\}\times\{1,...,d\}
,
\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\theta}]_{m}}[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})]_{n}|]<\infty
. This is true if, for each
j\in\{1,...,J-1\}
,

\mathbb{E}\left[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\frac{\pi_{a,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{b}(Y_{t};X_{b,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{b}),\bm{\upsilon}_{b})}{\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{k}(Y_{t};X_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})}\right|\right]<\infty

and

\mathbb{E}\left[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\right)\pi_{a,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{b}(Y_{t};X_{b,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{b}),\bm{\upsilon}_{b})}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{k}(Y_{t};X_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})\right)^{2}}\right|\right]<\infty

for all
a,b\in\{1,...,J\}
and

\mathbb{E}\left[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{J}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{a}(Y_{t};X_{a,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{a}),\bm{\upsilon}_{a})}{\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{k}(Y_{t};X_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})}\right|\right]<\infty

and

\mathbb{E}\left[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\left|\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\right)\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{la}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{a}(Y_{t};X_{a,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{a}),\bm{\upsilon}_{a})\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{k}(Y_{t};X_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})\right)^{2}}\right|\right]<\infty

for all
a\in\{1,...,J\}
and
b\in\{1,...,d_{a}\}
; see Section
C
for details. Condition (i) thus follows since, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|^{m_{j}}]<\infty
,
\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}]<\infty
, and
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{m_{j}}]<\infty
by assumption. To show Conditions (ii) and (iii), note that, for each
(n,m)\in\{1,...,J-1\}\times\{1,...,d\}
,

\displaystyle\left[(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}})^{(r)}(\mathbf{x};\bm{\theta})\right]_{(n-1)d+m}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{J-1}\left(\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{j}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}^{(r)}(\bm{\pi}_{t-r\mid t-r}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}^{(r)}(\bm{\pi}_{t-r\mid t-r}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}\right)\left[\mathbf{x}\right]_{(j-1)d+m}

\displaystyle+\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}^{(r)}(\bm{\pi}_{t-r\mid t-r}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}

for all
r\in\mathbb{N}
and
\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{(J-1)d}
. Note that

||\bm{\phi}_{t}^{(r)}(\mathbf{x};\bm{\theta})-\bm{\phi}_{t}^{(r)}(\mathbf{y};\bm{\theta})||_{2}\leq\Lambda(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{(r)};\bm{\theta})||\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{y}||_{2}

for all
r\in\mathbb{N}
and
\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\in\mathcal{S}
so, for each
j,n\in\{1,...,J-1\}
,

\left|\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{j}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}^{(r)}(\bm{\pi}_{t-r\mid t-r}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}^{(r)}(\bm{\pi}_{t-r\mid t-r}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}\right|\leq\Lambda(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{(r)};\bm{\theta})

for all
r\in\mathbb{N}
; see
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
for a similar argument. Hence, for each
(n,m)\in\{1,...,J-1\}\times\{1,...,d\}
,

\left|\left[(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}})^{(r)}(\mathbf{x};\bm{\theta})\right]_{(n-1)d+m}-\left[(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}})^{(r)}(\mathbf{y};\bm{\theta})\right]_{(n-1)d+m}\right|\leq\Lambda(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{(r)};\bm{\theta})\sum_{j=1}^{J}\left|\left[\mathbf{x}\right]_{(j-1)d+m}-\left[\mathbf{y}\right]_{(j-1)d+m}\right|

for all
r\in\mathbb{N}
and
\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{(J-1)d}
. Thus,

\Lambda((\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}})^{(r)};\bm{\theta})\leq C\Lambda(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{(r)};\bm{\theta})

for all
r\in\mathbb{N}
. Conditions (ii) and (iii) thus follow by using the same arguments as in the proof of Lemma
2
.

To show that
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}^{2}]<\infty
, we have, under Conditions (i)-(iii), that

\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}((\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}})^{(k)}(\mathbf{V}_{t-k}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})-(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}})^{(k)}(\mathbf{0};\bm{\theta})),

where, by convention,
(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}})^{(0)}(\mathbf{v};\bm{\theta})=\mathbf{v}
and

[\mathbf{V}_{t-k}(\bm{\theta})]_{(n-1)d+m}=\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t-k}(\bm{\pi}_{t-k-1\mid t-k-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n},\quad(n,m)\in\{1,...,J-1\}\times\{1,...,d\}.

Thus,

||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}^{2}\leq\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}||(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}})^{(k)}(\mathbf{V}_{t-k}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})-(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}})^{(k)}(\mathbf{0};\bm{\theta})||_{2}||(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}})^{(l)}(\mathbf{V}_{t-l}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})-(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}})^{(l)}(\mathbf{0};\bm{\theta})||_{2}.

Note that, by using the same arguments as above and in the proof of Lemma
2
, there exists an
\alpha\in(0,1)
such that

\sup_{\underset{\mathbf{x}\neq\mathbf{y}}{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{(J-1)d}}}\frac{||(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}})^{(k)}(\mathbf{x};\bm{\theta})-(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}})^{(k)}(\mathbf{y};\bm{\theta})||_{2}}{||\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{y}||_{2}}\leq C\alpha^{k}

for all
k\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
. Thus,

||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}^{2}\leq C\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\alpha^{k}||\mathbf{V}_{t-k}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}\alpha^{l}||\mathbf{V}_{t-l}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}.

Hence, we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}^{2}\right]\leq C\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\mathbf{V}_{t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}^{2}\right],

so
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}^{2}]<\infty
since, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|^{2}||\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}]<\infty
and
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{2}]<\infty
by assumption.

Moreover,
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}
is a stochastic process taking values in
\mathbb{R}^{(J-1)d}
given by

\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})=\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})

with

\displaystyle\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{(n-1)d+m}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{J-1}\left(\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{j}}\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}}\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}\right)\left[\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\right]_{(j-1)d+m}

\displaystyle+\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m}}\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n},\quad(n,m)\in\{1,...,J-1\}\times\{1,...,d\},

where
\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{0\mid 0}(\bm{\theta})
is given. The fact that also
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
follows also from Theorem 2.10 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
if

(i)

\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}]<\infty
,

(ii)

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}}(\mathbf{0};\bm{\theta})-\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}}(\mathbf{0};\bm{\theta})||_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
, and

(iii)

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\Lambda(\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}}-\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}};\bm{\theta})\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
.

Condition (i) follows since
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}^{2}]<\infty
. Condition (ii) follows if, for each
(n,m)\in\{1,...,J-1\}\times\{1,...,d\}
,
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}|\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\theta}]_{m}}[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{[\bm{\theta}]_{m}}[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})]_{n}|\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
. This is true if, for each
j\in\{1,...,J-1\}
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\hat{\pi}_{a,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{b}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{b,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{b}),\bm{\upsilon}_{b})}{\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{lk}\hat{\pi}_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{k}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{\pi_{a,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{b}(Y_{t};X_{b,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{b}),\bm{\upsilon}_{b})}{\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{k}(Y_{t};X_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})}\right|\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0\hskip 9.24994pt\text{as}\hskip 9.24994ptt\rightarrow\infty

and

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}p_{ij}\hat{\pi}_{i,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{j}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\right)\hat{\pi}_{a,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{b}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{b,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{b}),\bm{\upsilon}_{b})}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{lk}\hat{\pi}_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{k}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})\right)^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\right)\pi_{a,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{b}(Y_{t};X_{b,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{b}),\bm{\upsilon}_{b})}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{k}(Y_{t};X_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})\right)^{2}}\right|\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0\hskip 9.24994pt\text{as}\hskip 9.24994ptt\rightarrow\infty

for all
a,b\in\{1,...,J\}
and

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{J}p_{ij}\hat{\pi}_{i,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{a}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{a,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{a}),\bm{\upsilon}_{a})}{\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{lk}\hat{\pi}_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{k}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{J}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{a}(Y_{t};X_{a,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{a}),\bm{\upsilon}_{a})}{\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{k}(Y_{t};X_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})}\right|\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0\hskip 9.24994pt\text{as}\hskip 9.24994ptt\rightarrow\infty

and

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}p_{ij}\hat{\pi}_{i,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{j}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\right)\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{la}\hat{\pi}_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{a}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{a,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{a}),\bm{\upsilon}_{a})\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{lk}\hat{\pi}_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{k}(Y_{t};\hat{X}_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})\right)^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{j}(Y_{t};X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})\right)\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{la}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{a}(Y_{t};X_{a,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{a}),\bm{\upsilon}_{a})\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})f_{k}(Y_{t};X_{k,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{k}),\bm{\upsilon}_{k})\right)^{2}}\right|\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0\hskip 9.24994pt\text{as}\hskip 9.24994ptt\rightarrow\infty

for all
a\in\{1,...,J\}
and
b\in\{1,...,d_{a}\}
; see Section
C
for details once again. Condition (ii) thus follows by using the same arguments as in the proof of Lemma
2
since
(Y_{t})_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary by Assumption
1
. Moreover, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|^{m_{j}}]<\infty
,
\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}]<\infty
,
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{m_{j}}]<\infty
,
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|^{m_{j}}]<\infty
, and
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{m_{j}}]<\infty
by assumption. Finally, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}|\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})-X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
by Lemma
1
,
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})-\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
by Lemma
2
, and, for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
,
\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}||\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\hat{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})-\nabla_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}X_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
by Lemma
3
.

To show Condition (iii), by using the same arguments as above once again,

\Lambda(\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}}-\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}};\bm{\theta})\leq C\Lambda(\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}-\bm{\phi}_{t};\bm{\theta}).

Condition (iii) thus follows by using the same arguments as in the proof of Lemma
2
.
∎

B.4
Proof of Lemma
6

The conclusion follows from Lemmas
2
and
B.1
and the following lemma.

Lemma B.2
.

Assume that Assumptions
1
-
4
,
8
-
10
, and the conditions in Lemmas
1
-
4
and
B.1
hold. Moreover, assume that for each
j\in\{1,...,J\}
, there exists an
m_{j}>0
such that

(i)

\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}^{m_{j}}]<\infty
,

(ii)

\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}|\bar{\nabla}_{x_{j}x_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})|^{m_{j}}]<\infty
,

(iii)

\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}x_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2}^{m_{j}}]<\infty
, and

(iv)

\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\in\bar{\bm{\Upsilon}}_{j}}\sup_{x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}_{j}}||\bar{\nabla}_{\bm{\upsilon}_{j}\bm{\upsilon}_{j}x_{j}}\log f_{j}(Y_{t};x_{j},\bm{\upsilon}_{j})||_{2,2}^{m_{j}}]<\infty
.

Then,
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}
with
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}]<\infty
and

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0\quad\text{as}\quad t\rightarrow\infty

for any initialisation
\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{0\mid 0}(\bm{\theta})\in\mathbb{R}^{(J-1)d^{2}}
.

Proof.

First,
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is a stochastic process taking values in
\mathbb{R}^{(J-1)d^{2}}
given by

\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})=\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}\bm{\pi}}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})

with

\displaystyle\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}\bm{\pi}}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{(n-1)d^{2}+(m_{1}-1)d+m_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{J-1}\left(\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{j}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\cdot\left[\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\right]_{(j-1)d^{2}+(m_{1}-1)d+m_{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{J-1}\left(\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{j}\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{2}}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{2}}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}\right)\nabla_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{1}}}\left[\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\right]_{j}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{J-1}\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}\Bigl[\left(\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{j}\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{i}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{j}\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-\left(\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{i}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}\right)\Bigr]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\cdot\nabla_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{1}}}\left[\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\right]_{j}\nabla_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{2}}}\left[\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\right]_{i}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}\left(\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{1}}\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{i}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{1}}\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}\right)\nabla_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{2}}}\left[\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\right]_{i}

\displaystyle+\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{1}}\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{2}}}\left[\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\pi}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n},\quad(n,m_{1},m_{2})\in\{1,...,J-1\}\times\{1,...,d\}\times\{1,...,d\};

see Section
C
for details. Let

\Lambda(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}\bm{\pi}};\bm{\theta})=\sup_{\underset{\mathbf{x}\neq\mathbf{y}}{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{(J-1)d^{2}}}}\frac{||\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}\bm{\pi}}(\mathbf{x};\bm{\theta})-\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}\bm{\pi}}(\mathbf{y};\bm{\theta})||_{2}}{||\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{y}||_{2}}.

The fact that
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
is stationary and ergodic for all
\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}
with
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}]<\infty
follows from Theorem 2.10 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
if

(i)

\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}\bm{\pi}}(\mathbf{0};\bm{\theta})||_{2}]<\infty
,

(ii)

\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\Lambda(\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}\bm{\pi}};\bm{\theta})]<\infty
, and

(iii)

there exists an
r\in\mathbb{N}
such that
-\infty\leq\mathbb{E}[\log\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\Lambda((\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}\bm{\pi}})^{(r)};\bm{\theta})]<0
.

The proof follows the same lines as the proof of Lemma
B.1
, so we omit the details.

Moreover,
(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta}))_{t\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}
is a stochastic process taking values in
\mathbb{R}^{(J-1)d^{2}}
given by

\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})=\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}\bm{\pi}}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})

with

\displaystyle\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}\bm{\pi}}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{(n-1)d^{2}+(m_{1}-1)d+m_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{J-1}\left(\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{j}}\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}}\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\cdot\left[\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\right]_{(j-1)d^{2}+(m_{1}-1)d+m_{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{J-1}\left(\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{j}\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{2}}}\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{2}}}\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}\right)\nabla_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{1}}}\left[\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\right]_{j}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{J-1}\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}\Bigl[\left(\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{j}\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{i}}\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{j}\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}}\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-\left(\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{i}}\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}}\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}\right)\Bigr]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\cdot\nabla_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{1}}}\left[\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\right]_{j}\nabla_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{2}}}\left[\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\right]_{i}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}\left(\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{1}}\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{i}}\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}-\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{1}}\left[\bm{\pi}\right]_{J}}\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n}\right)\nabla_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{2}}}\left[\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta})\right]_{i}

\displaystyle+\bar{\nabla}_{\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{1}}\left[\bm{\theta}\right]_{m_{2}}}\left[\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}(\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t-1\mid t-1}(\bm{\theta});\bm{\theta})\right]_{n},\quad(n,m_{1},m_{2})\in\{1,...,J-1\}\times\{1,...,d\}\times\{1,...,d\},

where
\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{0\mid 0}(\bm{\theta})
is given. The fact that also
\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\hat{\bm{\pi}}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
follows also from Theorem 2.10 in
Straumann and Mikosch (2006)
if

(i)

\mathbb{E}[\log^{+}\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\nabla_{\bm{\theta}\bm{\theta}}\bm{\pi}_{t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})||_{2}]<\infty
,

(ii)

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}||\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}\bm{\pi}}(\mathbf{0};\bm{\theta})-\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}\bm{\pi}}(\mathbf{0};\bm{\theta})||_{2}\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
, and

(iii)

\sup_{\bm{\theta}\in\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}\Lambda(\hat{\bm{\phi}}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}\bm{\pi}}-\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\bm{\pi}\bm{\pi}};\bm{\theta})\overset{e.a.s.}{\rightarrow}0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
.

We omit the details once again for the same reason as above.
∎

Appendix C
Supplementary Material

In the following,
\pi_{j,t\mid t}
denotes
{\pi}_{j,t\mid t}(\bm{\theta})
and
{f}_{j,t}
denotes
f_{j}(Y_{t};{X}_{j,t}(\bm{\upsilon}_{j}),\bm{\upsilon}_{j})
.

For each
j\in\{1,...,J-1\}
,
\nabla_{p_{ab}}\pi_{j,t\mid t}
is given by

\displaystyle\nabla_{p_{ab}}\pi_{j,t\mid t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1_{\{j=b\}}f_{j,t}\pi_{a,t-1\mid t-1}}{\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}f_{j,t}(p_{ij}-p_{Jj})\nabla_{p_{ab}}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}}{\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)(f_{b,t}-f_{J,t})\pi_{a,t-1\mid t-1}}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{p_{ab}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

for all
a\in\{1,...,J\}
and
b\in\{1,...,J-1\}
and
\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}\pi_{j,t\mid t}
is given by

\displaystyle\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}\pi_{j,t\mid t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1_{\{j=a,a\in\{1,...,J-1\}\}}\sum_{i=1}^{J}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}}{\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}f_{j,t}(p_{ij}-p_{Jj})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}}{\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{a,t}p_{la}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

for all
a\in\{1,...,J\}
and

.

Moreover, for each
j\in\{1,...,J-1\}
,
\nabla_{p_{ab}p_{\alpha\beta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t}

\displaystyle-\frac{1_{\{j=b\}}f_{j,t}\pi_{a,t-1\mid t-1}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{p_{\alpha\beta}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}f_{j,t}(p_{ij}-p_{Jj})\nabla_{p_{\alpha\beta}}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)(f_{b,t}-f_{J,t})\pi_{a,t-1\mid t-1}}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1_{\{j=\beta\}}f_{j,t}\pi_{\alpha,t-1\mid t-1}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{p_{ab}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}f_{j,t}(p_{ij}-p_{Jj})\nabla_{p_{\alpha\beta}}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{p_{ab}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(1_{\{l=\alpha,k=\beta\}}-1_{\{J=\alpha,k=\beta\}})\nabla_{p_{ab}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot(f_{\beta,t}-f_{J,t})\pi_{\alpha,t-1\mid t-1}

\displaystyle+2\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{p_{ab}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{p_{\alpha\beta}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)

for all

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}f_{j,t}(p_{ij}-p_{Jj})\nabla_{p_{ab}}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}1_{\{j=\alpha,\alpha\in\{1,...,J-1\}\}}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)(f_{b,t}-f_{J,t})\pi_{a,t-1\mid t-1}}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}f_{j,t}(p_{ij}-p_{Jj})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)(f_{b,t}-f_{J,t})\pi_{a,t-1\mid t-1}}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)(1_{\{b=\alpha\}}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{b,t}-1_{\{J=\alpha\}}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{J,t})\pi_{a,t-1\mid t-1}}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)(f_{b,t}-f_{J,t})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{a,t-1\mid t-1}}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle+2\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)(f_{b,t}-f_{J,t})\pi_{a,t-1\mid t-1}\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{\alpha,t}p_{l\alpha}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{3}}

\displaystyle+2\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)(f_{b,t}-f_{J,t})\pi_{a,t-1\mid t-1}}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}1_{\{j=\alpha,\alpha\in\{1,...,J-1\}\}}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{p_{ab}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}f_{j,t}(p_{ij}-p_{Jj})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{p_{ab}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(1_{\{k=\alpha\}}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{k,t}-1_{\{J=\alpha\}}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{p_{ab}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{p_{ab}[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle+2\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{p_{ab}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{\alpha,t}p_{l\alpha}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)

\displaystyle+2\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{p_{ab}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)

for all
a\in\{1,...,J\}
,
b\in\{1,...,J-1\}
,
\alpha\in\{1,...,J\}
, and
\beta\in\{1,...,d_{\alpha}\}
, and
\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t}
is given by

\displaystyle\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{j,t\mid t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1_{\{j=a,a\in\{1,...,J-1\}\}}\sum_{i=1}^{J}1_{\{j=\alpha,\alpha\in\{1,...,J-1\}\}}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}}{\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1_{\{j=a,a\in\{1,...,J-1\}\}}\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{j,t}(p_{ij}-p_{Jj})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}}{\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(1_{\{j=a,a\in\{1,...,J-1\}\}}\sum_{i=1}^{J}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{\alpha,t}p_{l\alpha}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(1_{\{j=a,a\in\{1,...,J-1\}\}}\sum_{i=1}^{J}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}1_{\{j=\alpha,\alpha\in\{1,...,J-1\}\}}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{j,t}(p_{ij}-p_{Jj})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}}{\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}f_{j,t}(p_{ij}-p_{Jj})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}}{\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}f_{j,t}(p_{ij}-p_{Jj})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{\alpha,t}p_{l\alpha}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}f_{j,t}(p_{ij}-p_{Jj})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}1_{\{j=\alpha,\alpha\in\{1,...,J-1\}\}}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{a,t}p_{la}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}f_{j,t}(p_{ij}-p_{Jj})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{a,t}p_{la}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}1_{\{a=\alpha\}}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{a,t}p_{la}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{a,t}(p_{la}-p_{Ja})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle+2\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{a,t}p_{la}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{\alpha,t}p_{l\alpha}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{3}}

\displaystyle+2\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}f_{a,t}p_{la}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}1_{\{j=\alpha,\alpha\in\{1,...,J-1\}\}}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J-1}f_{j,t}(p_{ij}-p_{Jj})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(1_{\{k=\alpha\}}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{k,t}-1_{\{J=\alpha\}}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle+2\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\sum_{l=1}^{J}\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}f_{\alpha,t}p_{l\alpha}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)

\displaystyle+2\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{J}f_{j,t}p_{ij}\pi_{i,t-1\mid t-1}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{a}]_{b}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)}{\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J}\sum_{l=1}^{J}f_{k,t}p_{lk}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\sum_{k=1}^{J-1}\sum_{l=1}^{J-1}(f_{k,t}-f_{J,t})(p_{lk}-p_{Jk})\nabla_{[\bm{\upsilon}_{\alpha}]_{\beta}}\pi_{l,t-1\mid t-1}\right)

for all

,

,
\alpha\in\{1,...,J\}
, and
